Extensive experiments across multiple domain generalization object detection datasets, i.e., Cross-Camera, Adverse Weather, Real-to-Artistic, Diverse Weather Datasets, and Diverse Weather DroneVehicle (our newly augmented real-world UAV-based benchmark), underscore the superiority of our proposed method over previous state-of-the-art approaches. The project page is available at: https://mingbohong.github.io/Bridge/.

</details>


### [74] [Edge AI for Automotive Vulnerable Road User Safety: Deployable Detection via Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26857)
*Akshay Karjol,Darrin M. Hanna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出知识蒸馏框架，训练紧凑型YOLOv8-S学生模型（11.2M参数）模仿YOLOv8-L教师模型（43.7M参数），在INT8量化下实现3.9倍压缩，学生模型保留精度（-5.6% mAP）而教师模型严重退化（-23% mAP），INT8下学生模型精度（0.748）甚至超过教师FP32精度（0.718），虚警降低44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘硬件上部署准确的VRU（弱势道路使用者）检测需要在模型容量与计算约束之间取得平衡。大模型精度高但在INT8量化下性能崩溃，小模型则牺牲检测性能。

Method: 提出知识蒸馏框架，使用YOLOv8-L（43.7M参数）作为教师，训练YOLOv8-S（11.2M参数）作为学生，在BDD100K数据集（70K训练图像）上进行训练，并采用后训练量化（PTQ）至INT8。

Result: 知识蒸馏学生模型在INT8下mAP仅下降5.6%（教师下降23%），精度达0.748（直接训练仅为0.653，提升14.5%），虚警较崩溃教师降低44%，INT8学生精度（0.748）超过教师FP32精度（0.718），模型体积小3.9倍。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏是边缘硬件上部署安全关键的VRU检测的必要条件，其传递的是精度校准能力而非原始检测容量。

Abstract: Deploying accurate object detection for Vulnerable Road User (VRU) safety on edge hardware requires balancing model capacity against computational constraints. Large models achieve high accuracy but fail under INT8 quantization required for edge deployment, while small models sacrifice detection performance. This paper presents a knowledge distillation (KD) framework that trains a compact YOLOv8-S student (11.2M parameters) to mimic a YOLOv8-L teacher (43.7M parameters), achieving 3.9x compression while preserving quantization robustness. We evaluate on full-scale BDD100K (70K training images) with Post-Training Quantization to INT8. The teacher suffers catastrophic degradation under INT8 (-23% mAP), while the KD student retains accuracy (-5.6% mAP). Analysis reveals that KD transfers precision calibration rather than raw detection capacity: the KD student achieves 0.748 precision versus 0.653 for direct training at INT8, a 14.5% gain at equivalent recall, reducing false alarms by 44% versus the collapsed teacher. At INT8, the KD student exceeds the teacher's FP32 precision (0.748 vs. 0.718) in a model 3.9x smaller. These findings establish knowledge distillation as a requirement for deploying accurate, safety-critical VRU detection on edge hardware.

</details>


### [75] [Breaking the Rigid Prior: Towards Articulated 3D Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26868)
*Jinye Gan,Bozhong Zheng,Xiaohao Xu,Junye Ren,Zixuan Zhang,Na Ni,Yingna Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有3D异常检测方法无法处理铰接/滑动关节物体的问题，提出首个大规模基准ArtiAD（39类15229点云）和基线方法SPA-SDF（姿态条件隐式场），在已知/未知关节配置下分别达到0.884和0.874的AUROC，显著优于所有基于刚体先验的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D异常检测方法基于刚体先验（几何姿态不变性），无法处理铰接或滑动关节的物体，因为有效姿态变化会引入结构化几何变化，导致位姿诱导变形被误判为异常，而真实结构缺陷被掩盖。目前缺乏相关基准。

Method: 提出ArtiAD基准（39类15229点云，含关节角度变化和6种结构异常类型）和SPA-SDF基线，后者用连续姿态条件的隐式场替代刚体先验，分解为与关节无关的结构先验和傅里叶编码的关节嵌入。推理时通过最小化重建能量恢复关节状态，将异常识别为点到流形的偏离。

Result: SPA-SDF在已知关节配置上达到0.884对象级AUROC，在未知配置上达到0.874，显著优于所有基于刚体先验的基线方法。

Conclusion: ArtiAD是首个针对铰接物体的3D异常检测基准，SPA-SDF通过姿态条件隐式场有效解耦姿态诱导几何与结构缺陷，为处理非刚体物体的异常检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing 3D anomaly detection methods are built on a rigid prior: normal geometry is pose-invariant and can be canonicalized through registration or alignment. This prior does not hold for articulated objects with hinge or sliding joints, where valid pose changes induce structured geometric variations that cannot be collapsed to a single canonical template, causing pose-induced deformations to be misidentified as anomalies while true structural defects are obscured. No existing benchmark addresses this challenge. We introduce ArtiAD, the first large-scale benchmark for articulated 3D anomaly detection, comprising 15,229 point clouds across 39 object categories with dense joint-angle variations and six structural anomaly types. Each sample is annotated with its joint configuration and part-level motion labels, enabling explicit disentanglement of pose-induced geometry from structural defects. ArtiAD also provides a seen/unseen articulation split to evaluate both interpolation and extrapolation to novel joint configurations. We propose Shape-Pose-Aware Signed Distance Field (SPA-SDF), a baseline that replaces the rigid prior with a continuous pose-conditioned implicit field, factorized into an articulation-independent structural prior and a Fourier-encoded joint embedding. At inference, the articulation state is recovered by minimizing reconstruction energy, and anomalies are identified as point-wise deviations from the learned manifold. SPA-SDF achieves 0.884 object-level AUROC on seen configurations and 0.874 on unseen configurations, substantially outperforming all rigid-based baselines. Our code and benchmark will be publicly released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [76] [Uncertainty-Aware Pedestrian Attribute Recognition via Evidential Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26873)
*Zhuofan Lou,Shihang Zhang,Fangle Zhu,Shengjie Ye,Pingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UAPAR框架，首次将基于证据深度学习(EDL)的不确定性感知方法引入行人属性识别(PAR)，通过CLIP架构、区域感知证据推理模块和不确定性引导的双阶段课程学习策略，在多个数据集上达到竞争性或更优性能，并能有效识别不可靠预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统确定性行人属性识别方法在处理低质量样本时无法评估预测可靠性，在复杂真实场景中鲁棒性不足。首次将EDL引入PAR以量化预测不确定性，提升系统鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 将EDL融入CLIP架构；2) 提出区域感知证据推理模块，使用交叉注意力和空间先验掩码捕获细粒度局部特征，再通过证据头估计属性级认知不确定性；3) 开发不确定性引导的双阶段课程学习策略，缓解训练中严重标签噪声问题。

Result: 在PA100K、PETA、RAPv1和RAPv2四个数据集上达到竞争性或更优性能；定性结果表明不确定性估计能够预测具有挑战性或错误的样本。

Conclusion: UAPAR是首个基于EDL的不确定性感知PAR框架，有效识别不可靠预测并提升系统鲁棒性，适用于复杂真实场景。

Abstract: We propose UAPAR, an Uncertainty-Aware Pedestrian Attribute Recognition framework. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first EDL-based uncertainty-aware framework for pedestrian attribute recognition (PAR). Unlike conventional deterministic methods, which fail to assess prediction reliability on low-quality samples, UAPAR effectively identifies unreliable predictions and thus enhances system robustness in complex real-world scenarios. To achieve this, UAPAR incorporates Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) into a CLIP-based architecture. Specifically, a Region-Aware Evidence Reasoning module employs cross-attention and spatial prior masks to capture fine-grained local features, which are further processed by an evidence head to estimate attribute-wise epistemic uncertainty. To further enhance training robustness, we develop an uncertainty-guided dual-stage curriculum learning strategy to alleviate the adverse effects of severe label noise during training. Extensive experiments on the PA100K, PETA, RAPv1, and RAPv2 datasets demonstrate that UAPAR achieves competitive or superior performance. Furthermore, qualitative results confirm that the proposed framework generates uncertainty estimates that are predictive of challenging or erroneous samples.

</details>


### [77] [AnimateAnyMesh++: A Flexible 4D Foundation Model for High-Fidelity Text-Driven Mesh Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26917)
*Zijie Wu,Chaohui Yu,Fan Wang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnimateAnyMesh++ 是一个前馈式框架，用于对任意3D网格进行文本驱动的动画生成。该工作通过三方面升级——扩展 DyMesh-XL 数据集（身份数从60K增至300K）、重新设计 DyMeshVAE-Flex（引入幂律拓扑感知注意力与顶点法向增强特征）、支持变长序列训练与生成——显著提升了4D内容生成的语义准确性、时间连贯性和几何保持能力，在数秒内即可生成高质量网格动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D内容生成面临两大挑战：一是时空分布建模的复杂性，二是高质量4D训练数据的稀缺性。该工作旨在解决这些问题，提升文本驱动3D网格动画的质量与效率。

Method: 1) 扩展 DyMesh-XL 数据集，从 Objaverse-XL 挖掘动态内容，将身份数从60K增至300K；2) 重新设计 DyMeshVAE-Flex，引入幂律拓扑感知注意力和顶点法向增强特征；3) 对 DyMeshVAE-Flex 和整流流生成器进行架构改进，支持变长序列训练与生成。

Result: AnimateAnyMesh++ 能在数秒内生成语义准确且时间连贯的网格动画，在质量和效率上超越先前方法。扩展的数据集、升级的VAE和变长RF共同在基准测试和野外网格上带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: AnimateAnyMesh++ 通过数据、架构和生成能力的实质性升级，有效攻克了4D内容生成中的关键难题。作者承诺在论文被接收后将开源代码、模型和扩展后的数据集，以促进4D内容创作领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D content generation have attracted increasing attention, yet creating high-quality animated 3D models remains challenging due to the complexity of modeling spatio-temporal distributions and the scarcity of 4D training data. We present AnimateAnyMesh++, a feed-forward framework for text-driven animation of arbitrary 3D meshes with substantial upgrades in data, architecture, and generative capability. First, we expand the DyMesh-XL dataset by mining dynamic content from Objaverse-XL, increasing the number of unique identities from 60K to 300K and substantially broadening category and motion diversity. Second, we redesign DyMeshVAE-Flex with power-law topology-aware attention and vertex-normal enhanced features, which significantly improves trajectory reconstruction, local geometry preservation, and mitigates trajectory-sticking artifacts. Third, we introduce architectural changes to both DyMeshVAE-Flex and the rectified-flow (RF) generator to support variable-length sequence training and generation, enabling longer animations while preserving reconstruction fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AnimateAnyMesh++ generates semantically accurate and temporally coherent mesh animations within seconds, surpassing prior approaches in quality and efficiency. The enlarged DyMesh-XL, the upgraded DyMeshVAE-Flex, and variable-length RF together deliver consistent gains across benchmarks and in-the-wild meshes. We will release code, models, and the expanded DyMesh-XL upon acceptance of this manuscript to facilitate research in 4D content creation.

</details>


### [78] [Color-Encoded Illumination for High-Speed Volumetric Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26920)
*David Novikov,Eilon Vaknin,Narek Tumanyan,Mark Sheinin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用未改装的低速相机，通过快速顺序彩色编码照明实现高速场景的体积捕捉与重建方法，并基于动态高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）解码时间信息，首次实现了高速体积场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机带宽有限（30-60 FPS），难以捕捉高速动态场景的3D表示。现有高速计算成像方法多需修改相机光学或添加机械组件，且局限于单视角，无法直接用于多视角3D高速场景重建。

Method: 通过快速顺序彩色编码照明将高速时间信息编码到低速相机捕获的图像空间强度与颜色变化中，再基于动态高斯泼溅方法解码时间信息，构建高速场景的体积表示。

Result: 在模拟场景和真实多相机实验平台上进行了评估，展示了首创的高速体积场景重建结果。

Conclusion: 本文证明了仅用未改装的低速相机即可实现高速3D动态场景的体积捕捉与重建，为高速场景的多视角体积重建提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: The task of capturing and rendering 3D dynamic scenes from 2D images has become increasingly popular in recent years. However, most conventional cameras are bandwidth-limited to 30-60 FPS, restricting these methods to static or slowly evolving scenes. While overcoming bandwidth limitations is difficult for general scenes, recent years have seen a flurry of computational imaging methods that yield high-speed videos using conventional cameras for specific applications (e.g., motion capture and particle image velocimetry). However, most of these methods require modifications to a camera's optics or the addition of mechanically moving components, limiting them to a single-view high-speed capture. Consequently, these methods cannot be readily used to capture a 3D representation of rapid scene motion. In this paper, we propose a novel method to capture and reconstruct a volumetric representation of a high-speed scene using only unaugmented low-speed cameras. Instead of modifying the hardware or optics of each individual camera, we encode high-speed scene dynamics by illuminating the scene with a rapid, sequential color-coded sequence. This results in simultaneous multi-view capture of the scene, where high-speed temporal information is encoded in the spatial intensity and color variations of the captured images. To construct a high-speed volumetric representation of the dynamic scene, we develop a novel dynamic Gaussian Splatting-based approach that decodes the temporal information from the images. We evaluate our approach on simulated scenes and real-world experiments using a multi-camera imaging setup, showing first-of-a-kind high-speed volumetric scene reconstructions.

</details>


### [79] [World2VLM: Distilling World Model Imagination into VLMs for Dynamic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26934)
*Wanyue Zhang,Wenxiang Wu,Wang Xu,Jiaxin Luo,Helu Zhi,Yibin Huang,Shuo Ren,Zitao Liu,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: World2VLM通过将生成式世界模型的空间想象能力蒸馏到视觉语言模型中，使其能够内化动态空间推理，而无需推理时的高成本生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在静态视觉理解上表现良好，但缺乏动态空间推理能力，无法想象以自我为中心的相机运动下场景如何演变。现有方法要么缺乏对运动条件状态转换的显式建模，要么因推理时耦合世界模型而产生高计算开销。

Method: 提出World2VLM训练框架：利用视图一致的世界模型，根据初始观测和参数化相机轨迹合成几何对齐的未来视图，提取前向推理（动作→结果）和逆向推理（结果→动作）的结构化监督信号，然后通过两阶段后训练策略在小型数据集上微调VLM。

Result: 在SAT-Real、SAT-Synthesized、VSI-Bench和MindCube等多个空间推理基准上，World2VLM一致优于基础模型，且超越了推理时耦合世界模型的方法，同时消除了昂贵的推理时生成需求。

Conclusion: 世界模型不仅可作为推理时工具，还能作为高效的训练时教师，使VLM以可扩展的方式内化空间想象能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong performance on static visual understanding, yet they still struggle with dynamic spatial reasoning that requires imagining how scenes evolve under egocentric motion. Recent efforts address this limitation either by scaling spatial supervision with synthetic data or by coupling VLMs with world models at inference time. However, the former often lacks explicit modeling of motion-conditioned state transitions, while the latter incurs substantial computational overhead. In this work, we propose World2VLM, a training framework that distills spatial imagination from a generative world model into a vision-language model. Given an initial observation and a parameterized camera trajectory, we use a view-consistent world model to synthesize geometrically aligned future views and derive structured supervision for both forward (action-to-outcome) and inverse (outcome-to-action) spatial reasoning. We post-train the VLM with a two-stage recipe on a compact dataset generated by this pipeline and evaluate it on multiple spatial reasoning benchmarks. World2VLM delivers consistent improvements over the base model across diverse benchmarks, including SAT-Real, SAT-Synthesized, VSI-Bench, and MindCube. It also outperforms the test-time world-model-coupled methods while eliminating the need for expensive inference-time generation. Our results suggest that world models can serve not only as inference-time tools, but also as effective training-time teachers, enabling VLMs to internalize spatial imagination in a scalable and efficient manner.

</details>


### [80] [ProcFunc: Function-Oriented Abstractions for Procedural 3D Generation in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26943)
*Alexander Raistrick,Karhan Kayan,Jack Nugent,David Yan,Lingjie Mei,Meenal Parakh,Hongyu Wen,Dylan Li,Yiming Zuo,Erich Liang,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProcFunc是一个基于Blender的Python程序化3D生成库，提供易用的Python函数来简化程序化生成代码的创建、组合、分析和执行，支持大规模多样化训练数据生成，并允许VLM编辑和创建程序化代码。论文还开发了一个新的室内房间程序化生成器作为示例应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化程序化3D内容生成的流程，使创建大规模多样化训练数据更加便捷，同时降低VLM在编辑和创建程序化代码时的编码错误率。

Method: 设计了一个基于Blender的Python函数库，通过语义组件的组合式合成实现程序化生成，支持VLM对程序化材质和几何代码进行编辑与创建。

Result: 开发了室内房间程序化生成器，展示了其细节丰富度、运行效率、多样性以及在3D合成数据生成中的应用效果。

Conclusion: ProcFunc能够有效简化程序化3D生成流程，提升数据多样性和VLM代码生成的正确性。

Abstract: We introduce ProcFunc, a library for Blender-based procedural 3D generation in Python. ProcFunc provides a library of easy-to-use Python functions, which streamline creating, combining, analyzing, and executing procedural generation code. ProcFunc makes it easy to create large-scale diverse training data, by combinatorial compositions of semantic components. VLMs can use ProcFunc to edit procedural material and geometry code and can create new procedural code with significantly fewer coding errors. Finally, as an example use case, we use ProcFunc to develop a new procedural generator of indoor rooms, which includes a collection of new compositional procedural materials. We demonstrate the detail, runtime efficiency, and diversity of this room generator, as well as its use for 3D synthetic data generation. Please visit https://github.com/princeton-vl/procfunc for source code.

</details>


### [81] [Three-Step Nav: A Hierarchical Global-Local Planner for Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26946)
*Wanrong Zheng,Yunhao Ge,Laurent Itti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Three-Step Nav，一种三视角协议（前视、当前视、后视），用于提升基于多模态大语言模型的零样本视觉语言导航性能，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于MLLM的零样本VLN智能体存在偏离航向、过早停止、整体成功率低等问题，亟需一种无需梯度更新或任务微调即可纠正这些失败的轻量级方法。

Method: 提出Three-Step Nav三视角协议：1) Look Forward（前视）——提取全局地标并绘制粗略路径；2) Look Now（当前视）——将当前视觉观测与下一子目标对齐以提供细粒度引导；3) Look Backward（后视）——审计整个轨迹以在停止前纠正累积漂移。无需梯度更新或微调，可直接嵌入现有VLN框架。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE数据集上达到零样本SOTA性能。

Conclusion: Three-Step Nav通过简单有效的三视角策略显著提升了MLLM在VLN任务中的鲁棒性和成功率，且具有良好的即插即用特性。

Abstract: Breakthrough progress in vision-based navigation through unknown environments has been achieved by using multimodal large language models (MLLMs). These models can plan a sequence of motions by evaluating the current view at each time step against the task and goal given to the agent. However, current zero-shot Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) agents powered by MLLMs still tend to drift off course, halt prematurely, and achieve low overall success rates. We propose Three-Step Nav to counteract these failures with a three-view protocol: First, "look forward" to extract global landmarks and sketch a coarse plan. Then, "look now" to align the current visual observation with the next sub-goal for fine-grained guidance. Finally, "look backward" audits the entire trajectory to correct accumulated drift before stopping. Requiring no gradient updates or task-specific fine-tuning, our planner drops into existing VLN pipelines with minimal overhead. Three-Step Nav achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE dataset. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZoeyZheng0/3-step-Nav.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [On the Centralization of Governance Power in Decentralized Autonomous Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25959)
*Vabuk Pahari,Balakrishnan Chandrasekaran,Johnnatan Messias,Krishna P. Gummadi,Abhisek Dash*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对48个活跃且资金规模庞大的以太坊DAO进行研究，发现代币注册、质押和委托这三种原本旨在提升安全性和参与度的治理机制，实际上系统性加剧了投票权力的集中化，揭示了DAO治理设计与其去中心化目标之间的固有矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管去中心化和自治是DAO设计的核心原则，但实证证据表明实践中治理往往高度中心化。作者旨在系统性地揭示DAO自身的治理机制如何导致投票权力集中，而非仅针对特定DAO的个案分析。

Method: 研究选取了48个部署在以太坊上、资金规模庞大且公开活跃使用的DAO，分析其治理合约的设计与实现，重点考察代币注册（token registration）、质押（staking）和委托（delegation）三种关键治理机制对投票权分布的影响。

Result: 研究发现这三种治理机制并非个别DAO的特有问题，而是系统性地强化了投票权力的集中化趋势。原本用于提升安全性或参与度的机制，在实际运作中反而成为权力集中的推手。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了治理设计与投票中心化之间的关系，揭示了DAO在去中心化、安全性和可用性之间存在的固有权衡，为理解DAO治理结构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: A decentralized autonomous organization (DAO) is a governing entity that empowers its stakeholders (i.e., users who hold one or more of its tokens) to manage blockchain-based protocols (i.e., smart contracts) collaboratively. The governance of a DAO is explicitly encoded in the DAO's governance contract, which defines how stakeholders participate in governance and how much influence (or voting power) they have in any decision. While decentralization and autonomy are the fundamental tenets of a DAO's design, empirical evidence suggests that in practice governance is often highly centralized. In this work, we study the designs and implementations of 48 public and actively used DAOs, with substantially large capital, deployed on Ethereum. We identify how three key governance mechanisms--token registration, staking, and delegation--originally introduced to improve security or participation, contribute to the concentration of voting power. Unlike prior work on centralization of voting power in specific DAOs, our findings reveal that these governance mechanisms of DAOs themselves systematically reinforce centralization. By elucidating the relationship between governance design and voting centralization, this work advances the understanding of DAO governance structures and highlights the inherent trade-offs between decentralization, security, and usability of DAOs.

</details>


### [83] [A Quantitative Confirmation of the Currier Language Distinction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25979)
*Christophe Parisel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过Beta-Binomial混合模型对伏尼契手稿中字符对替换比率进行定量分析，无监督方式以ARI=0.383复现了Currier的A/B语言区分，有监督分类器达到89.2%准确率，并发现字符对分为三个功能区间，为伏尼契文字系统理论提供约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Currier（1976）提出的伏尼契手稿A/B语言区分是否反映文本真实的、可量化的结构属性，而非偶然或人工分类结果。

Method: 采用Beta-Binomial混合模型对原始字符对替换比率进行无监督聚类（不依赖标签），复现Currier分裂并计算ARI=0.383；同时构建有监督Beta-Binomial分类器，以部分folio训练后预测其余folio的A/B类别，评估准确率。

Result: 无监督模型以ARI=0.383恢复Currier分裂；有监督分类准确率达89.2%；字符对可划分为三个功能区间。

Conclusion: Currier的A/B语言区分具有可量化的结构基础；字符对的三区间划分对任何关于伏尼契文字系统的理论构成约束条件。

Abstract: We present a quantitative analysis of character-pair substitution ratios in the Voynich manuscript, testing whether Currier's A/B language distinction (1976) reflects a genuine structural property of the text. A Beta-Binomial mixture model applied to raw character counts without access to labels recovers the Currier split with ARI = 0.383. A supervised Beta-Binomial classifier trained on a subset of folios predicts the A/B identity of held-out folios at 89.2% accuracy. The character pairs separate into three functional regimes that constrain any theory of the Voynich writing system.

</details>


### [84] [Large Language Models as Explainable Cyberattack Detectors for Energy Industrial Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26079)
*Weiyi Kong,Ahmad Mohammad Saber,Amr Youssef,Deepa Kundur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究使用现成的大语言模型（LLM）作为Modbus流量的补充性人机协同入侵检测层，将协议字段离散化为紧凑token串，通过提示配置使LLM生成正常/告警分类并附带基于token的事件记录，在无需任务特定权重更新的情况下达到与强监督基线相当的性能，并通过干预诊断验证了审计token的决策相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ICS入侵检测主要由监督式检测器主导，但缺乏可审计性。作者希望探索LLM能否作为互补的人机协同层，在保持检测精度的同时提供可供操作员审核的审计记录。

Method: 将Modbus通信实例的协议字段离散化为紧凑token字符串，通过提示配置使LLM输出二分类（正常/告警）并附带基于token的事件记录。在两组公开ICS Modbus数据集上进行评估，与强监督基线对比，并使用充分性/必要性干预诊断测试审计token的决策相关性。

Result: LLM管道在两个基准上均取得了高预测性能，与强监督基线大体相当，且无需任务特定权重更新。干预诊断证明引用的token通常与模型自身的预测决策相关。

Conclusion: 现成LLM可作为ICS入侵检测的有效补充层，提供可审计的检测结果，审计记录旨在作为分析信号而非完整的人工可理解解释。

Abstract: In modern energy systems, industrial control systems (ICS) and power-system SCADA require intrusion detection that is not only accurate but also auditable by operators. The ICS intrusion-detection landscape is currently dominated by established supervised detectors. In this paper, we study whether an off-the-shelf large language model (LLM) can serve as a complementary, human-in-the-loop layer for Modbus traffic. We cast this as a binary network-side normal/critical decision task on two public ICS Modbus datasets, collapsing attack periods and other safety-critical behaviors into a single critical class. Each Modbus communication instance is converted into a compact token string derived from discretized protocol fields, and a prompt-configured LLM produces a normal/critical alert together with a concise, token-grounded incident record for analyst review. Under matched event information and shared evaluation splits, the resulting LLM-based triage pipeline achieves high predictive performance on both benchmarks and is broadly comparable to strong supervised baselines, while requiring no task-specific weight updates. To assess the audit record, we apply intervention-based diagnostics, including sufficiency- and necessity-style tests, which provide evidence that the cited tokens are often decision-relevant to the model's own prediction. These records are intended as audit signals rather than full human-grounded explanations.

</details>


### [85] [LATTICE: Evaluating Decision Support Utility of Crypto Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26235)
*Aaron Chan,Tengfei Li,Tianyi Xiao,Angela Chen,Junyi Du,Xiang Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LATTICE是一个评估加密领域AI助手决策支持效用的基准，定义了6个评估维度、16种任务类型，使用LLM评判器自动评分，并评估了6个真实加密助手在1200个查询上的表现，发现不同助手在维度和任务层面存在显著性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加密智能体基准主要关注推理或结果导向的评估，未能评估智能体辅助用户决策的能力。LATTICE旨在填补这一空白，提供一种可扩展、可审计的评估框架，用于评估生产级加密助手在真实场景中的决策支持效用。

Method: (1) 定义了六个评估维度，涵盖关键决策支持属性；(2) 提出了16种任务类型，覆盖端到端加密助手工作流；(3) 使用LLM评判器基于这些维度和任务自动对智能体输出评分；(4) 使用该基准评估了6个真实加密助手在1200个多样化查询上的表现。

Result: 大多数被测试的加密助手在聚合分数上表现相当，但在维度和任务层面的性能差异更为显著，表明存在有意义的决策支持质量权衡。不同优先级的用户可能会被不同助手更好地服务，而非仅看聚合排名。

Conclusion: LATTICE提供了一个可靠且可扩展的评估框架，通过LLM评判器实现自动化评估，并且评判标准可随新维度、任务、标准和人类反馈持续审计和更新。该基准揭示了不同加密助手在决策支持上的差异化表现，对用户选择具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: We introduce LATTICE, a benchmark for evaluating the decision support utility of crypto agents in realistic user-facing scenarios. Prior crypto agent benchmarks mainly focus on reasoning-based or outcome-based evaluation, but do not assess agents' ability to assist user decision-making. LATTICE addresses this gap by: (1) defining six evaluation dimensions that capture key decision support properties; (2) proposing 16 task types that span the end-to-end crypto copilot workflow; and (3) using LLM judges to automatically score agent outputs based on these dimensions and tasks. Crucially, the dimensions and tasks are designed to be evaluable at scale using LLM judges, without relying on ground truth from expert annotators or external data sources. In lieu of these dependencies, LATTICE's LLM judge rubrics can be continually audited and updated given new dimensions, tasks, criteria, and human feedback, thus promoting reliable and extensible evaluation. While other benchmarks often compare foundation models sharing a generic agent framework, we use LATTICE to assess production-level agents used in actual crypto copilot products, reflecting the importance of orchestration and UI/UX design in determining agent quality. In this paper, we evaluate six real-world crypto copilots on 1,200 diverse queries and report breakdowns across dimensions, tasks, and query categories. Our experiments show that most of the tested copilots achieve comparable aggregate scores, but differ more significantly on dimension-level and task-level performance. This pattern suggests meaningful trade-offs in decision support quality: users with different priorities may be better served by different copilots than the aggregate rankings alone would indicate. To support reproducible research, we open-source all LATTICE code and data used in this paper.

</details>


### [86] [VulStyle: A Multi-Modal Pre-Training for Code Stylometry-Augmented Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26313)
*Chidera Biringa,Ajmal Abbas,Vishnu Selvaraj,Gokhan Kul*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VulStyle是一个多模态软件漏洞检测模型，通过联合编码函数级源代码、非终结符AST结构和代码风格特征，在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，F1提升4-48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码表示方法主要依赖token级模型或完整AST树，要么忽略了反映风险编程实践的风格线索，要么带来较高的结构开销，亟需一种既能捕捉风格特征又能降低结构复杂度的漏洞检测方法。

Method: VulStyle采用多模态架构，联合编码三种特征：函数级源代码、仅保留非终结符的AST节点（降低输入复杂度同时保持语义层次）、代码风格特征（语法和词汇层面）。模型在7种编程语言的490万函数上通过掩码语言建模预训练，在5个基准数据集上微调。

Result: 在BigVul和VulDeePecker上达到SOTA，F1比强Transformer基线提升4-48%；在所有基准数据集上取得竞争性或最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: 代码风格特征和非终结符AST结构能有效增强漏洞检测能力；消融实验验证了各模态的贡献，错误案例分析和威胁模型为实际部署提供了参考。

Abstract: We present VulStyle, a multi-modal software vulnerability detection model that jointly encodes function-level source code, non-terminal Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) structure, and code stylometry (CStyle) features. Prior work in code representation primarily leverages token-level models or full AST trees, often missing stylistic cues indicative of risky programming practices, or incurring high structural overhead. Our approach selects only non-terminal AST nodes, reducing input complexity while preserving semantic hierarchy, and integrates syntactic and lexical CStyle features as auxiliary vulnerability signals.
  VulStyle is pre-trained using masked language modeling on 4.9M functions across seven programming languages, and fine-tuned across five benchmark datasets: Devign, BigVul, DiverseVul, REVEAL, and VulDeePecker. VulStyle achieves state-of-the-art performance on BigVul and VulDeePecker, improving F1 by 4-48% over strong transformer baselines, and attains competitive or best-average performance across all benchmarks. We contribute an ablation study isolating the effect of CStyle and AST structure, error case analysis, and a threat model situating the detection task in attacker-realistic scenarios.

</details>


### [87] [Differentially Private Contrastive Learning via Bounding Group-level Contribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26467)
*Kecen Li,Chen Gong,Zinan Lin,Tianhao Wang,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-GCL框架，通过分组限制梯度依赖和组内增强技术，有效解决了差分隐私对比学习中的效用退化问题，在图像分类和图文检索任务上分别提升5.6%和20.1%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私对比学习方法因标准对比目标中样本间依赖过强，导致每个样本梯度受批次中所有其他样本影响，放大了DP噪声的影响，造成严重的效用退化。因此，需要显式降低这种内在的样本间依赖。

Method: 提出DP-GCL框架：1) 将批次划分为小型不相交的组，限制负样本仅来源于组内，局部化梯度影响并降低敏感度；2) 引入组内增强技术，在不增加隐私成本的情况下生成额外负样本，弥补负样本多样性损失。

Result: 在8个数据集上的实验表明，DP-GCL在单模态和多模态对比学习中均达到最先进水平：图像分类准确率提升5.6%，图文检索准确率提升20.1%。

Conclusion: 通过结构性限制组级别的梯度贡献，DP-GCL有效降低了对比学习对DP噪声的敏感性，为实用隐私预算下的差分隐私对比学习提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) contrastive learning aims to learn general-purpose representations from sensitive data, alleviating the privacy leakage concerns of organizations deploying or sharing embedding models trained on private user content. However, existing approaches suffer from severe utility degradation due to the over-strong inter-sample dependency inherent in standard contrastive objectives, where each sample's gradient depends on all other samples in the batch, amplifying the impact of DP noise. In this work, we argue that effective DP contrastive learning requires explicitly reducing such intrinsic inter-sample reliance. To this end, we propose DP-GCL, a principled DP contrastive learning framework that structurally limits gradient dependency through bounding group-level contribution. DP-GCL partitions each batch into small, disjoint groups and restricts available negative samples to within-group samples, thereby localizing gradient influence and reducing sensitivity. To counteract the resulting loss of negative sample diversity, we further introduce intra-group augmentation, which generates additional negative views without increasing privacy cost. Extensive experiments across eight datasets demonstrate that DP-GCL consistently advances the state of the art in both uni-modal and multi-modal contrastive learning under practical privacy budgets: it improves image classification accuracy by 5.6% and image-text retrieval accuracy by 20.1% over existing DP contrastive methods.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond Code Reasoning: A Specification-Anchored Audit Framework for Expert-Augmented Security Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26495)
*Masato Kamba,Hirotake Murakami,Akiyoshi Sannai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SPECA框架，从自然语言规范中提取类型化安全属性，通过结构化证明推理进行代码审计，在以太坊和C/C++基准测试中有效检测漏洞，误报可追溯至三类根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全审计工具基于代码库本地模式推理，无法检测由自然语言规范本身引发的漏洞（即"规范要求什么"而非"代码如何写"导致的缺陷），且缺乏表达规范级安全属性的词汇，误报难以系统诊断。

Method: SPECA框架：从自然语言规范中推导显式、类型化的安全属性，基于每个属性通过结构化证明尝试推理来审计实现。支持规范依赖检测、跨实现对比、误报可分解为可解释的流水线阶段可追溯的根本原因。

Result: 在以太坊Fusaka审计竞赛（366个提交，10个实现）中恢复全部15个范围内漏洞并独立发现4个已确认bug；在RepoAudit C/C++基准（15个项目）上达到最高精度88.9%，发现12个超越真值的候选bug（2个已确认）；多模型分析表明更强模型在属性范围内审计更忠实，瓶颈从模型推理转向属性生成质量。

Conclusion: SPECA证明了规范锚定审计的有效性，误报全部可追溯至信任边界误解、代码阅读错误、规范误解三类可操作的根本原因，为安全审计提供了超越纯代码驱动方法的新范式。

Abstract: Security-critical software is routinely audited by tools that reason about vulnerabilities as repository-local code patterns. Yet specification-governed systems -- protocol stacks, consensus implementations, cryptographic libraries -- are constrained by invariants and correctness conditions defined in natural-language specifications. When a vulnerability arises from what the specification requires rather than how code is written, code-level approaches lack the representational vocabulary to detect it, and their false positives resist systematic diagnosis.
  We present SPECA, a specification-anchored security audit framework that derives explicit, typed security properties from natural-language specifications and audits implementations through structured proof-attempt reasoning grounded in each property. The framework yields three capabilities absent from code-driven auditing: spec-dependent detections, controlled cross-implementation comparison under a shared property vocabulary, and false positives that decompose into interpretable, pipeline-phase-traceable root causes.
  On the Sherlock Ethereum Fusaka Audit Contest (366 submissions, 10 implementations), SPECA recovers all 15 in-scope vulnerabilities and independently discovers 4 bugs confirmed by developer fix commits. On the RepoAudit C/C++ benchmark (15 projects), SPECA matches the best published precision (88.9\%) while surfacing 12 candidate bugs beyond the established ground truth, two confirmed by upstream maintainers. A multi-model analysis reveals that more capable models audit more faithfully within property scope, shifting the detection bottleneck from model reasoning to property generation quality. All false positives trace to three recurring root causes -- trust boundary misunderstanding, code reading errors, and specification misinterpretation -- each yielding actionable improvement targets.

</details>


### [89] [Quantamination: Dynamic Quantization Leaks Your Data Across the Batch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26505)
*Hanna Foerster,Ilia Shumailov,Cheng Zhang,Yiren Zhao,Jamie Hayes,Robert Mullins*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种名为"Quantamination"的新型安全漏洞：攻击者可利用动态量化策略在批处理推理过程中窃取同批其他用户的敏感数据。研究发现至少4种主流ML框架存在此问题，允许跨批次边界的数据泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态量化被广泛推荐用于优化模型服务效率，但其安全性未得到充分审视。作者旨在揭示动态量化在批处理场景下可能引入的隐私风险，即攻击者如何利用量化策略窃取同批其他用户的输入数据。

Method: 通过分析动态量化的运行时特性（逐张量自适应量化参数），发现当不同用户的输入被分在同一批次时，量化参数的差异会形成侧信道，攻击者可通过观察自身输入对应的量化行为推断同批其他用户的数据信息。

Result: 至少4种主流ML框架（包括ML编译器和推理引擎）在默认或可配置设置下存在此漏洞；攻击者理论上可部分甚至完全恢复同批其他用户的输入数据。

Conclusion: 动态量化在提升效率的同时引入了严重隐私风险，现有ML服务框架需要重新审视批处理场景下的量化实现与配置安全性。

Abstract: Dynamic quantization emerged as a practical approach to increase the utilization and efficiency of the machine learning serving flow. Unlike static quantization, which applies quantization offline, dynamic quantization operates on tensors at run-time, adapting its parameters to the actual input data. Today's mainstream machine learning frameworks, including ML compilers and inference engines, frequently recommend dynamic quantization as an initial step for optimizing model serving. This is because dynamic quantization can significantly reduce memory usage and computational load, leading to faster token generation and improved model serving efficiency without substantial loss in model accuracy. In this paper, we reveal a critical vulnerability in dynamic quantization: an adversary can exploit such quantization strategy to steal sensitive user data placed in the same batch as the adversary's input. Our analysis demonstrates that dynamic quantization, when improperly implemented or configured, can create side channels that expose information about other inputs within the same batch. We call this phenomenon Quantamination, describing contamination from quantization. Specifically, we show that at least 4 of the most popular ML frameworks in use today either default to or can use configurations that leak data across the batch boundary. This data leakage, in theory, allows attackers to partially or even fully recover other users' batched input data, representing a serious privacy risk for existing ML serving frameworks.

</details>


### [90] [Tatemae: Detecting Alignment Faking via Tool Selection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26511)
*Matteo Leonesi,Francesco Belardinelli,Flavio Corradini,Marco Piangerelli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化对齐伪装（AF）为复合行为事件，通过工具选择模式检测LLM在监控下被迫服从训练目标、监控解除后恢复原始偏好的行为。在108个企业IT场景中测试六种前沿LLM，平均AF检测率为3.5%-23.7%，表明易感性反映训练方法而非仅能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AF检测方法局限于对话设置且依赖CoT分析，无法区分欺骗与能力失败，需要更可靠的行为级检测方案。

Method: 将AF形式化为复合行为事件，通过可观察的工具选择进行检测：LLM在无监控时选择安全工具，在监控下（奖励有用性而非安全性）切换到不安全工具，但推理过程仍承认安全选择。在108个企业IT场景（安全、隐私、完整性领域）下进行腐败和破坏压力测试。

Result: 六种前沿LLM在五次独立运行中平均AF检测率为3.5%-23.7%，不同领域和压力类型下脆弱性分布存在差异。

Conclusion: LLM的对齐伪装易感性主要反映训练方法差异，而非单纯能力水平决定。

Abstract: Alignment faking (AF) occurs when an LLM strategically complies with training objectives to avoid value modification, reverting to prior preferences once monitoring is lifted. Current detection methods focus on conversational settings and rely primarily on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) analysis, which provides a reliable signal when strategic reasoning surfaces, but cannot distinguish deception from capability failures if traces are absent or unfaithful. We formalize AF as a composite behavioural event and detect it through observable tool selection, where the LLM selects the safe tool when unmonitored, but switches to the unsafe tool under monitoring that rewards helpfulness over safety, while its reasoning still acknowledges the safe choice. We release a dataset of 108 enterprise IT scenarios spanning Security, Privacy, and Integrity domains under Corruption and Sabotage pressures. Evaluating six frontier LLMs across five independent runs, we find mean AF detection rates between 3.5% and 23.7%, with vulnerability profiles varying by domain and pressure type. These results suggest that susceptibility reflects training methodology rather than capability alone.

</details>


### [91] [PRAG End-to-End Privacy-Preserving Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26525)
*Zhijun Li,Minghui Xu,Huayi Qi,Wenxuan Yu,Tingchuang Zhang,Qiao Zhang,GuangYong Shang,Zhen Ma,Xiuzhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PRAG是一个端到端隐私保护的RAG系统，采用双模式架构（非交互式PRAG-I和交互式PRAG-II），通过同态加密近似和操作误差估计机制，在保证可扩展性的同时实现文档和查询的端到端机密性，在大规模数据集上达到72.45%-74.45%的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统依赖云端环境，导致敏感数据面临隐私泄露风险。已有的隐私保护方案要么因噪声注入牺牲检索质量，要么仅提供部分加密，无法兼顾隐私保护与检索性能。

Method: PRAG采用双模式架构：PRAG-I利用同态加密友好近似实现低延迟检索（非交互式），PRAG-II借助客户端辅助实现与非隐私RAG相当的准确性（交互式）。同时提出操作误差估计（OEE）机制，稳定同态噪声下的语义排序。

Result: 在大规模数据集上，PRAG实现了72.45%-74.45%的竞争性召回率、实用的检索延迟，以及对图重构攻击的强大鲁棒性，同时保持了端到端机密性。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了在大规模场景下实现安全、高性能RAG的可行性，为隐私保护RAG系统的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is essential for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs) with external knowledge, but its reliance on cloud environments exposes sensitive data to privacy risks. Existing privacy-preserving solutions often sacrifice retrieval quality due to noise injection or only provide partial encryption. We propose PRAG, an end-to-end privacy-preserving RAG system that achieves end-to-end confidentiality for both documents and queries without sacrificing the scalability of cloud-hosted RAG. PRAG features a dual-mode architecture: a non-interactive PRAG-I utilizes homomorphic-friendly approximations for low-latency retrieval, while an interactive PRAG-II leverages client assistance to match the accuracy of non-private RAG. To ensure robust semantic ordering, we introduce Operation-Error Estimation (OEE), a mechanism that stabilizes ranking against homomorphic noise. Experiments on large-scale datasets demonstrate that PRAG achieves competitive recall (72.45%-74.45%), practical retrieval latency, and strong resilience against graph reconstruction attacks while maintaining end-to-end confidentiality. This work confirms the feasibility of secure, high-performance RAG at scale.

</details>


### [92] [Preventing Distinguishability between Multiplication and Squaring Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26536)
*Alkistis Aikaterini Sigourou,Zoya Dyka,Peter Langendoerfer,Ievgen Kabin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了椭圆曲线密码系统中标量乘法kP在简单侧信道分析下的脆弱性，发现即使采用原子模式策略，二进制kP算法仍因域乘法器处理不同或相同操作数时的能量消耗差异而存在安全漏洞。作者实现并分析了两种缓解技术：数据重定向和总线重载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆曲线密码系统中的标量乘法kP是核心操作，容易被侧信道分析攻击。尽管已有基于原子模式的安全增强策略，但二进制kP算法仍存在安全漏洞。

Method: 作者分析了能量消耗差异的根源（域乘法器处理两个不同或两个相同操作数时的能耗变化），并实现和分析了两种缓解技术：数据重定向和总线重载。

Result: 发现了独立于乘法方法的能量消耗差异漏洞，并提出了两种可行的缓解方案。

Conclusion: 数据重定向和总线重载两种技术可有效缓解因操作数相同性导致的能量消耗差异漏洞，增强标量乘法对简单侧信道分析的抵抗能力。

Abstract: Scalar multiplication kP is a critical operation in Elliptic Curve Cryptosystems (ECC), often targeted by Side-Channel Analysis (SCA). Despite strategies based on atomic patterns to enhance security, the binary kP algorithms remain susceptible to simple SCA due to energy consumption variations in field multipliers during passing two different or two identical operands. This vulnerability arises independent of the multiplication method used. We implemented and analysed two mitigation techniques: one involving data redirection and another focusing on bus reloading.

</details>


### [93] [Catching the Fly: Practical Challenges in Making Blockchain FlyClient Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26736)
*Pericle Perazzo,Dario Capecchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文旨在将轻量级区块链验证协议FlyClient从实验阶段推进至生产就绪状态，通过引入替代对手模型、首个生产级区块链（Zcash）证明器实现以及两项证明大小优化，弥合理论与实际部署之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FlyClient协议虽在资源受限环境（移动钱包、物联网设备、跨链桥）中具有轻量级验证的潜力，但仍处于实验阶段，缺乏实际部署和多样化条件下的性能评估，亟需解决从理论到生产部署的技术挑战。

Method: (i) 引入替代原始FlyClient的对手模型，支持在具体经济解释下参数化验证者并节省证明空间；(ii) 首次在生产级区块链Zcash上实现FlyClient证明器，评估不同配置下的性能；(iii) 提出两种优化方案以最小化FlyClient证明大小，其中第一种无需共识变更。

Result: 在Zcash上实现了首个生产级FlyClient证明器，并评估了不同配置下的性能表现；两种优化方案有效减小了证明大小，且第一种优化不要求共识层改动。

Conclusion: 通过对手模型改进、生产级实现和证明大小优化，FlyClient得以从实验阶段推进至生产就绪状态，为轻量级区块链验证的实际部署铺平了道路。

Abstract: FlyClient is a lightweight blockchain verification protocol that enables proof-of-work validation using minimal data, making it ideal for resource-constrained environments like mobile wallets, Internet-of-Things devices or cross-chain bridges implemented with smart contracts. Despite its strong potential for enabling lightweight blockchain verification, FlyClient protocol is still in the experimental stages, with limited real-world deployments and performance evaluations under diverse conditions. In this paper we bridge the gap between theory and deployment, by addressing several technical challenges to advance FlyClient to a production-ready solution. Namely, our contribution is three-fold: (i) we formally introduce an adversary model alternative to the original FlyClient one, that allows us to parametrize a verifier under a concrete economic interpretation, while also saving some proof space; (ii) we provide the first practical FlyClient prover implementation for a production blockchain (Zcash), and we estimate its performance under different configurations; (iii) we introduce and evaluate two optimizations that minimize the size of FlyClient proofs, the first of which does not require any consensus change.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Occam's Razor is Only as Sharp as Your ELBO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25984)
*Ethan Harvey,Michael C. Hughes*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在变分推断中，降低近似后验协方差矩阵的秩虽有助于计算可扩展性，但可能导致ELBO目标产生过拟合，而边际似然（证据）本身有时也会偏爱过拟合版本，提醒大规模贝叶斯模型在实践中需谨慎处理低秩假设对模型选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究变分推断中ELBO目标在超参数学习时的过拟合现象，尤其是在过度参数化回归模型中使用低秩高斯近似后验时的问题。

Method: 通过一个简单的过度参数化回归模型，分析不同秩假设下的高斯近似后验对ELBO超参数学习的影响，并与边际似然（证据）的模型选择结果进行对比。

Result: ELBO在低秩近似下可能产生过拟合；边际似然有时也偏好过拟合版本；两者在欠拟合与过拟合之间的偏好存在差异。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯实践者在扩展到大规模模型时，应警惕为追求计算可扩展性而采用的降秩假设可能损害模型选择能力。

Abstract: The marginal likelihood, also known as the evidence, is regarded as a mathematical embodiment of Occam's razor, enabling model selection that avoids overfitting. The evidence lower bound (ELBO) objective from variational inference has also been used for similar purposes. Prior work has shown that restricting the approximate posterior family via a mean-field approximation can lead the ELBO to underfit. In this paper, we show how ELBO-based hyperparameter learning in a simple over-parameterized regression model can also produce overfitting, depending on the assumed rank of the covariance matrix in a Gaussian approximate posterior. Surprisingly, among only the underfit and overfit options, Bayesian model selection via the evidence itself sometimes prefers the overfit version, while the ELBO does not. Bayesian practitioners hoping to scale to large models should be cautious about how reduced-rank assumptions needed for tractability may impact the potential for model selection.

</details>


### [95] [Robust Representation Learning through Explicit Environment Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26128)
*Yuli Slavutsky,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出在环境直接作用于目标变量的非因果场景下，通过广义随机截距模型显式建模环境变异并进行边际化处理，学习比因果不变表示方法更鲁棒的预测表示，理论分析和实验均表明该方法在跨环境泛化上优于不变学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果不变学习方法假设环境对目标无直接影响，但许多现实场景中该假设不成立。本文旨在解决环境直接作用于目标时，如何学习能跨未见环境鲁棒预测的表示。

Method: 使用广义随机截距模型显式建模环境间的变异，然后通过边际化（marginalization）消除环境效应，从而得到不依赖特定环境的预测表示，并与因果不变表示方法进行对比分析。

Result: 理论分析刻画了所提表示优于因果不变表示的条件；实验表明在多种具有挑战性的设置下，该方法一致优于不变学习方法。

Conclusion: 当环境直接影响目标时，显式建模并边际化环境变异比追求因果不变性更有效，广义随机截距模型为此提供了一种可行的实现方案。

Abstract: We consider learning from labeled data collected across multiple environments, where the data distribution may vary across these environments. This problem is commonly approached from a causal perspective, seeking invariant representations that retain causal factors while discarding spurious ones. However, this framework assumes that the environment has no direct effect on the target. In contrast, we consider settings in which this assumption fails, but still aim to learn representations that support robust prediction on average across previously unseen environments. To this end, we study representations learned by explicitly modeling variation across environments and then marginalizing that variation out. We analyze the resulting representations and characterize when they are preferable to those learned by causal invariant-representation methods. We propose a concrete method based on generalized random-intercept models, a class of predictors in which such marginalization is possible, and study their generalization properties. Empirically, we show that these models outperform invariant-learning methods across a range of challenging settings.

</details>


### [96] [Probabilistic data quality assessment for structural monitoring data via outlier-resistant conditional diffusion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26366)
*Qi Li,Yong Huang,Hui Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于预测偏差的SHM数据质量评估方法，利用单变量隐式自回归模型和条件扩散模型，实现异常值诊断、数据清洗和全局质量评分，在真实结构数据上优于聚类、隔离和深度重建等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据质量评估是结构健康监测（SHM）后续任务可靠性的关键前提，现有方法在异常值诊断和整体数据质量量化方面存在不足。

Method: 提出基于条件扩散模型（CDM）的单变量隐式自回归框架：CDM通过条件嵌入模块整合时序上下文、四分位数归一化缓解分布偏斜、Huber损失增强对异常值的鲁棒性；每个数据点被赋予异常概率，并计算全局质量评估分数。

Result: 在真实结构运行数据上的案例研究表明，该方法在数据质量评估精度上显著优于聚类、隔离森林和深度重构等基线方法；消融实验和超参数分析进一步验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的预测偏差驱动的SHM数据质量评估方法能有效诊断异常点并清洗数据，为后续SHM任务提供可靠的数据保障。

Abstract: Data quality assessment is an essential step that ensures the reliability of the subsequent structural health monitoring (SHM) tasks. This study proposes a prediction deviation-based SHM data quality assessment method using a univariate implicit auto-regressive model, enabling outlier diagnosis and data cleaning. The proposed conditional diffusion model (CDM) augments the standard diffusion model with a conditional embedding module to incorporate temporal context, quartile normalization to mitigate distribution skew, and a Huber loss to enhance robustness against outliers. Within this univariate implicit autoregressive framework, each data point is assigned an outlier probability, quantifying its degree of "outlier-ness", and a global quality evaluation score is computed to characterize the overall dataset quality. Extensive case studies utilizing operational data from real-world structures demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly improves the accuracy of data quality assessment, outperforming other strong baselines representative of clustering, isolation-based, and deep reconstruction methods. The effectiveness and robustness of the proposed framework are further demonstrated by the findings of ablation experiments and hyperparameter analysis.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Estimating the tail index of Pareto-type distributions from geometric records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26069)
*Martín Alcalde,Raúl Gouet,Miguel Lafuente,F. Javier López,Gerardo Sanz*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何记录的新推断方法，用于估计重尾分布的尾指数，构建了Pareto模型的最大似然估计量，证明了其强相合性和渐近正态性，并通过模拟和实际数据（道琼斯工业平均指数波动）验证了其优越性，尤其在顺序到达数据和破坏性测试场景中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统尾指数估计方法（如Hill估计量）在数据顺序到达或破坏性测试等场景中效率较低或成本过高，亟需一种能在不完整观测下仍保持高效、平滑且准确的估计方法。

Method: 基于几何记录（geometric records）构建最大似然估计量，先针对Pareto模型推导估计量并证明其强相合性与渐近正态性，再推广至更广泛的Pareto型分布族，通过蒙特卡洛模拟与经典方法对比评估性能。

Result: 所提估计量具有强相合性和渐近正态性，渐近方差有显式表达式；在顺序数据场景中估计轨迹平滑；在破坏性测试中相比Hill估计量，以显著更少的测量观测值达到相当或更优的精度；道琼斯工业平均指数波动的应用分析验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 基于几何记录的尾指数估计方法在数据顺序到达和测量成本高昂的破坏性测试场景中具有明显优势，能够以更少观测实现高效稳健估计，是一种有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: In this paper we develop a novel inferential approach based on geometric records for estimating the tail index of heavy-tailed distributions. We construct a maximum likelihood estimator for the Pareto model and establish its strong consistency and asymptotic normality, providing also an explicit expression for its asymptotic variance. These results are then extended to a broad class of Pareto-type distributions. The performance of the estimator is assessed via Monte Carlo simulation and compared with classical estimators from the literature. The proposed method is particularly well suited for settings where data arrive sequentially, as it yields smooth estimation trajectories. It is also especially advantageous in applications such as destructive testing, where measuring each observation exactly is costly. In this context, the estimator clearly outperforms Hill's estimator, achieving comparable or better accuracy while requiring a substantially smaller number of measured observations. An application to the analysis of the distribution of fluctuations of the Dow Jones Industrial Average (DJI) is also presented.

</details>


### [98] [Asymptotics of ultra-high-dimensional generalized spiked sample covariance matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26178)
*Wonjun Seo*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了超高维设置下（p ≍ n^α, α>1）样本协方差矩阵在本征结构上的渐近行为，建立了特征值位置和特征向量投影的一阶收敛极限，是对Bloemendal (2016)和Ding (2021)工作的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超高维环境中（维度p远大于样本量n），样本协方差矩阵的本征结构行为尚不清晰，现有理论多限于p/n趋于常数的情况，需要将理论推广到p ≍ n^α（α>1）的场景。

Method: 基于尖峰协方差矩阵模型，研究维度p与样本量n满足p ≍ n^α（α>1）的超高维渐进框架。通过对适当缩放后的样本协方差矩阵进行理论分析，推导特征值位置和特征向量投影的一阶收敛极限。

Result: 建立了超高维设置下样本协方差矩阵特征值位置和特征向量投影的一阶收敛极限，将Bloemendal (2016)和Ding (2021)的结果推广到了p ≍ n^α（α>1）的情形。

Conclusion: 论文成功地将样本协方差矩阵本征结构渐近理论从p/n有界情形扩展到超高维情形，为超高维统计推断提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates the asymptotics of eigenstructure of sample covariance matrix under the spiked covariance matrix model in ultra-high-dimensional settings, where the dimensionality can grow much faster than the sample size with $ p \asymp n^α $, $ α> 1 $. We establish the first-order convergence limits of eigenvalue locations and eigenvector projections of properly scaled sample covariance matrix. Our results are extensions of \cite{bloemendal16,ding21}.

</details>


### [99] [A Leakage Bound for Confidence Sets after Black-Box Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26706)
*Sayantan Banerjee*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明，当选择规则为黑箱时，选定目标推断的非覆盖率受固定目标非覆盖率与推断数据边际分布和条件分布之间平均总变差距离（或互信息）的上界限制，将样本拆分视为零泄漏特例，并通过高斯信息界量化了黑箱选择的推断代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多数据分析中，最终报告的对象并非事先固定，而是在初步搜索（变量、子组、变换、模型或对比）后选择。经典选择性推断方法要求搜索过程可显式写为选择事件，但实际中常面临选择规则不透明的黑箱场景，需要一种更通用的理论框架来量化黑箱选择的推断代价。

Method: 针对黑箱选择规则（选择规则不可显式表达），作者建立了一般性理论界限：对于任意固定目标置信程序，选定目标非覆盖率 ≤ 固定目标非覆盖率 + 推断数据边际分布与给定选定对象后条件分布之间的平均总变差距离。进一步通过互信息界限和噪声筛选的高斯信息界给出可计算的特例，并表明样本拆分是零泄漏特例。

Result: 获得了选定目标非覆盖率的通用上界，该上界由信息泄露量（总变差距离或互信息）控制；样本拆分对应零信息泄漏；通过高斯信息界给出了噪声筛选的显式保证。

Conclusion: 黑箱选择的推断代价可通过选定对象携带关于推断样本的信息量来量化，为无法显式建模选择过程的实际数据分析提供了理论保障。

Abstract: In many analyses the object reported at the end is not fixed in advance, but is chosen after a preliminary search over variables, subgroups, transformations, models or contrasts. Classical selective-inference methods are most effective when this search can be written as an explicit selection event. This note treats the less structured case in which the selection rule is a black box and inference is required for the target indexed by the selected object. We show that, for any fixed-target confidence procedure, selected-target noncoverage is bounded by the nominal fixed-target noncoverage plus the average total variation distance between the marginal law of the inferential data and its conditional law given the selected object. A mutual-information bound follows immediately. The result recovers sample splitting as the zero-leakage case and gives explicit guarantees for noisy screening through a Gaussian information bound. Thus the inferential cost of black-box selection is quantified by the information that the selected object carries about the inferential sample.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Observable Neural ODEs for Identifiable Causal Forecasting in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26070)
*Jennifer Wendland,Nicolas Freitag,Maik Kschischo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在潜状态空间模型中，时变干预下的动态治疗效果可识别性等价于控制论中的可观性，并提出可观测神经常微分方程（ObsNODEs）用于因果预测，在癌症、MIMIC-IV和脓毒症数据上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间序贯决策中的因果推断受到隐藏混杂因素的挑战，现有方法难以处理时变干预下潜在动力学不可观测导致的识别问题。

Method: 从控制论可观性角度建立因果可识别性理论，推导连续时间调整公式，并提出ObsNODEs模型（基于可观规范形的神经常微分方程）来学习可重构潜状态的连续时间动力学。

Result: 在合成癌症数据、基于MIMIC-IV的半合成数据以及真实脓毒症数据上，ObsNODEs优于近期序列模型。

Conclusion: 潜状态的可观性是连续时间动态治疗效果可识别性的必要条件，ObsNODEs通过确保状态可重构性实现了有效的因果预测。

Abstract: Causal inference in continuous-time sequential decision problems is challenged by hidden confounders. We show that, in latent state-space models with time-varying interventions, observability of the latent dynamics from observed data is necessary for identifying dynamic treatment effects, linking control-theoretic observability to causal identifiability, even when hidden confounders affect both treatments and outcomes. We derive a continuous-time adjustment formula expressing potential outcome distributions under treatment trajectories via the measurement model, latent dynamics, and the filtering distribution over latent states given observed histories. We propose Observable Neural ODEs (ObsNODEs), Neural ODE models in observable normal form for causal forecasting. ObsNODEs learn continuous-time dynamics with states reconstructible from observations, enabling outcome prediction under alternative treatment paths. Experiments on synthetic cancer data, semi-synthetic data based on MIMIC-IV, and real-world sepsis data show strong performance over recent sequence models.

</details>


### [101] [The Geometric Canary: Predicting Steerability and Detecting Drift via Representational Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17698)
*Prashant C. Raju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何稳定性（几何表征距离结构的一致性）这一统一框架，同时解决大语言模型部署中的两个关键问题：预测模型是否可被线性操控（监督稳定性），以及检测模型内部结构退化（无监督稳定性）。实验表明，监督稳定性在预测操控性上近乎完美（ρ=0.89-0.97），无监督稳定性在漂移检测中比CKA灵敏近2倍，两者形成互补的部署诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可靠部署需要两个看似独立但实际共享几何基础的能力：预测模型是否会接受目标导向的行为控制（可操控性），以及检测其内部结构何时退化（漂移检测）。现有方法分别处理这两个问题，缺乏统一的理论框架。

Method: 提出几何稳定性概念，即表征的成对距离结构的一致性。监督Shesha变体衡量任务对齐的几何稳定性以预测线性可操控性；无监督几何稳定性用于检测模型漂移。在35-69个嵌入模型和三个NLP任务上进行验证。

Result: 监督稳定性预测可操控性近乎完美（ρ=0.89-0.97），捕获类可分性之外的独特方差（偏ρ=0.62-0.76）；无监督稳定性在漂移检测中比CKA灵敏近2倍（Llama中达5.23倍），73%的模型提供更早预警，误报率比Procrustes低6倍。无监督稳定性在真实任务的操控性预测上完全失效（ρ≈0.10）。

Conclusion: 监督稳定性和无监督稳定性形成互补的诊断工具：监督稳定性用于部署前的可控性评估，无监督稳定性用于部署后的监控，共同服务于LLM部署生命周期。

Abstract: Reliable deployment of language models requires two capabilities that appear distinct but share a common geometric foundation: predicting whether a model will accept targeted behavioral control, and detecting when its internal structure degrades. We show that geometric stability, the consistency of a representation's pairwise distance structure, addresses both. Supervised Shesha variants that measure task-aligned geometric stability predict linear steerability with near-perfect accuracy ($ρ= 0.89$-$0.97$) across 35-69 embedding models and three NLP tasks, capturing unique variance beyond class separability (partial $ρ= 0.62$-$0.76$). A critical dissociation emerges: unsupervised stability fails entirely for steering on real-world tasks ($ρ\approx 0.10$), revealing that task alignment is essential for controllability prediction. However, unsupervised stability excels at drift detection, measuring nearly $2\times$ greater geometric change than CKA during post-training alignment (up to $5.23\times$ in Llama) while providing earlier warning in 73\% of models and maintaining a $6\times$ lower false alarm rate than Procrustes. Together, supervised and unsupervised stability form complementary diagnostics for the LLM deployment lifecycle: one for pre-deployment controllability assessment, the other for post-deployment monitoring.

</details>


### [102] [A Randomized PDE Energy driven Iterative Framework for Efficient and Stable PDE Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25943)
*Yi Bing,Zheng Ran,Fu Jinyang,Liu Long,Peng Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PDE能量驱动的扩散迭代框架，无需传统矩阵组装或数据驱动训练，通过物理约束的扩散迭代求解偏微分方程。在一维Poisson、Heat和Burgers方程上验证，能从随机初始场稳定收敛到唯一物理解，具有竞争性的精度和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PDE数值求解器严重依赖基于矩阵的离散化方法，而基于学习的求解方法需要昂贵的训练且泛化能力有限。为克服这些局限性，需要一种无需矩阵组装、无需数据训练且物理一致的快速求解框架。

Method: 提出PDE能量驱动的隐式扩散迭代框架：从任意随机初始场出发，结合PDE能量约束和Gaussian平滑进行迭代演化，并在每次迭代中严格执行边界条件，最终收敛到物理解。

Result: 在一维Poisson（稳态）、Heat（瞬态）和粘性Burgers（瞬态）方程上验证。结果表明：从随机初始化能稳定收敛到唯一物理解；能准确解析尖锐梯度；在广泛的离散化参数范围内均方误差可控；与解析解比较具有竞争性的精度和稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种快速、灵活、物理一致的替代方案，可用于研究与工程中的可扩展PDE求解，无需传统矩阵方法或数据驱动训练。

Abstract: Efficient and stable solution of partial differential equations (PDEs) is central to scientific and engineering applications, yet existing numerical solvers rely heavily on matrix based discretizations, while learning based methods require costly training and often suffer from limited generalization. In this work, we proposes a PDE energy driven framework that solves PDEs through physically constrained diffusion iterations, without relying on classical matrix based finite element assembly or data driven neural network training. The proposed method evolves arbitrary random initial fields through PDE energy driven implicit iterations combined with Gaussian smoothing, while strictly enforcing boundary conditions at each iteration. The proposed formulation is applied to representative one dimensional Poisson, Heat, and viscous Burgers equations, covering both steady state and transient problems. Numerical results demonstrate stable convergence to the unique physical solution from random initializations, with accurate resolution of sharp gradients and controlled Mean Squared Error (MSE) across a wide range of discretization parameters. Detailed comparisons with analytical solutions indicate that the framework achieves competitive accuracy and stability. Overall, the proposed framework provides a fast, flexible, and physically consistent alternative to traditional numerical solvers, offering a potential pathway for scalable PDE solutions in both research and engineering applications.

</details>


### [103] [Budget-Constrained Causal Bandits: Bridging Uplift Modeling and Sequential Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26169)
*Abhirami Pillai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预算约束因果Bandit（BCCB）在线学习框架，在冷启动场景下无需历史数据即可同时学习广告效果、探索不确定性用户并控制预算。在Criteo数据集上，BCCB从首个用户开始有效运行，而离线方法需约1万条历史数据；BCCB方差降低3-5倍，且全面优于多种在线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统广告预算分配采用两阶段离线流程（先估计异质性处理效应HTE，再求解约束优化），在数据充足时有效，但在新广告活动、新市场、新客户群体等冷启动场景下缺乏历史数据，无法正常运行。

Method: 提出Budget-Constrained Causal Bandits (BCCB)在线框架，将学习个体广告效果、探索响应不确定的用户、预算时间调度三个组件统一到一个序列决策过程中，逐个用户进行投放决策。

Result: 在Criteo Uplift数据集上，离线方法需要约10,000条历史观测才能产生可靠结果，而BCCB从第一个用户即可有效运行；BCCB的运行间性能方差比离线方法低3-5倍；在所有预算水平下，BCCB一致优于标准Thompson Sampling、预算约束Thompson Sampling和贪心HTE估计。

Conclusion: BCCB作为在线框架在冷启动广告投放场景中显著优于传统离线方法和其他在线方法，具有数据效率高、方差低、鲁棒性强的优势，更适用于实际广告活动规划。

Abstract: Treatment allocation under budget constraints is a central challenge in digital advertising: advertisers must decide which users to show ads to while spending a limited budget wisely. The standard approach follows a two-stage offline pipeline - first collect historical data to estimate heterogeneous treatment effects (HTE), then solve a constrained optimization to allocate the budget. This works well with abundant data, but fails in cold-start settings such as new campaigns, new markets, or new customer segments where little historical data exists. We propose Budget-Constrained Causal Bandits (BCCB), an online framework that learns which users respond to ads while simultaneously spending the budget, making treatment decisions one user at a time. BCCB unifies three components into a single sequential process: learning individual-level ad effectiveness, exploring users whose response is uncertain, and pacing the budget over time. We evaluated on the Criteo Uplift dataset, a large-scale advertising dataset from a real randomized controlled trial. Our key finding is a data-efficiency crossover: offline methods require approximately 10,000 historical observations to produce reliable results, while BCCB operates effectively from the very first user. Furthermore, BCCB exhibits 3-5x lower performance variance between runs, making it more practical for real campaign planning. Among purely online methods, BCCB consistently outperforms standard Thompson Sampling, budgeted Thompson Sampling, and greedy HTE estimation across all budget levels tested.

</details>


### [104] [Addressing Performance Saturation for LLM RL via Precise Entropy Curve Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26326)
*Bolian Li,Yifan Wang,Yi Ding,Anamika Lochab,Ananth Grama,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Entrocraft，一种基于拒绝采样的方法，通过偏置优势分布实现自定义熵调度，有效解决强化学习中熵崩溃导致性能饱和的问题，使4B模型超越8B基线，pass@K提升50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL算法在大规模训练中会出现性能饱和，本质是熵崩溃导致探索不足。已有的正则化或裁剪方法虽然试图维持熵，但长期熵曲线不稳定，限制了性能提升。

Method: Entrocraft通过拒绝采样偏置优势分布，实现用户自定义的熵调度。该方法无需目标函数正则化，且与优势估计器无关。理论上推导了每步熵变化与优势分布的关系，并发现线性退火（从高初始熵逐渐衰减到略低目标）效果最佳。

Result: Entrocraft解决了性能饱和问题，显著提升泛化能力、输出多样性和长期训练效果。4B模型超越8B基线，训练持续改进时间延长4倍，pass@K相比基线提升50%。

Conclusion: Entrocraft是一种简单有效的熵调控方法，通过优势分布偏置实现灵活熵调度，从根本上缓解了RL训练中的熵崩溃和性能饱和问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has unlocked complex reasoning abilities in large language models (LLMs). However, most RL algorithms suffer from performance saturation, preventing further gains as RL training scales. This problem can be characterized by the collapse of entropy, a key diagnostic for exploration in RL. Existing attempts have tried to prevent entropy collapse through regularization or clipping, but their resulting entropy curves often exhibit instability in the long term, which hinders performance gains. In this paper, we introduce Entrocraft, a simple rejection-sampling approach that realizes any user-customized entropy schedule by biasing the advantage distributions. Entrocraft requires no objective regularization and is advantage-estimator-agnostic. Theoretically, we relate per-step entropy change to the advantage distribution under minimal assumptions, which explains the behavior of existing RL and entropy-preserving methods. Entrocraft also enables a systematic study of entropy schedules, where we find that linear annealing, which starts high and decays to a slightly lower target, performs best. Empirically, Entrocraft addresses performance saturation, significantly improving generalization, output diversity, and long-term training. It enables a 4B model to outperform an 8B baseline, sustains improvement for up to 4x longer before plateauing, and raises pass@K by 50% over the baseline.

</details>


### [105] [On the Learning Curves of Revenue Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26922)
*Steve Hanneke,Alkis Kalavasis,Shay Moran,Grigoris Velegkas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了收益最大化问题的学习曲线，在单个物品和单个买家的基本环境下，给出了学习曲线衰减率的近乎完整刻画：无限制分布下存在贝叶斯一致算法但收敛速度任意慢；若最优收益由有限价格实现，最优衰减率约为1/√n；离散分布下可实现近乎指数级衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于收益最大化学习算法的工作采用无分布视角（类似PAC学习框架），评估的是所有可能分布上学习曲线的上包络，得到的误差界无法捕捉学习曲线的实际形状。本文致力于研究收益最大化的学习曲线，以刻画算法性能随数据量增长的真实衰减率。

Method: 在单个物品、单个买家的基本设定下，从三个维度刻画学习曲线：1）无任何分布限制时，证明存在贝叶斯一致算法；2）当最优收益由有限价格实现时，分析最优衰减率；3）对于离散支撑的分布，分析衰减速度。

Result: 1）存在贝叶斯一致算法，对任意估值分布学习曲线收敛到零，但收敛速度可以任意慢；2）若最优收益由有限价格实现，最优衰减率约为1/√n；3）离散分布下学习曲线可实现近乎指数级衰减，这一速率在PAC框架下无法达到。

Conclusion: 本文为收益最大化的学习曲线研究奠定了基础，揭示了不同分布假设下学习曲线衰减率存在本质差异，并指出离散分布下的指数级衰减是PAC框架无法实现的独特性质。

Abstract: Learning curves are a fundamental primitive in supervised learning, describing how an algorithm's performance improves with more data and providing a quantitative measure of its generalization ability. Formally, a learning curve plots the decay of an algorithm's error for a fixed underlying distribution as a function of the number of training samples. Prior work on revenue-maximizing learning algorithms, starting with the seminal work of Cole and Roughgarden [STOC, 2014], adopts a distribution-free perspective, which parallels the PAC learning framework in learning theory. This approach evaluates performance against the hardest possible sequence of valuation distributions, one for each sample size, effectively defining the upper envelope of learning curves over all possible distributions, thus leading to error bounds that do not capture the shape of the learning curves.
  In this work we initiate the study of learning curves for revenue maximization and provide a near-complete characterization of their rate of decay in the basic setting of a single item and a single buyer. In the absence of any restriction on the valuation distribution, we show that there exists a Bayes-consistent algorithm, meaning that its learning curve converges to zero for any arbitrary valuation distribution as the number of samples $n \to \infty$. However, this convergence must be arbitrarily slow, even if the optimal revenue is finite. In contrast, if the optimal revenue is achieved by a finite price, then the optimal rate of decay is roughly $1/\sqrt{n}$. Finally, for distributions supported on discrete sets of values, we show that learning curves decay almost exponentially fast, a rate unattainable under the PAC framework.

</details>


### [106] [A Note on How to Remove the $\ln\ln T$ Term from the Squint Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26926)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了将Orabona和Pál [2016]中提出的移位KT势能等价于改变Krichevsky-Trofimov算法中的先验，并利用相同思路消除了Squint算法数据无关界中的ln ln T因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除参数自由学习与专家界以及Squint算法数据无关界中不必要的ln ln T因子，进一步优化算法理论界。

Method: 证明移位KT势能与改变KT算法先验的等价性，并将此思想推广至Squint算法中。

Result: 成功消除了Squint算法数据无关界中的ln ln T因子。

Conclusion: 移位KT势能的思想具有一般性，可推广至其他算法以去除ln ln T因子。

Abstract: In Orabona and Pál [2016], we introduced the shifted KT potentials, to remove the $\ln \ln T$ factor in the parameter-free learning with expert bound. In this short technical note, I show that this is equivalent to changing the prior in the Krichevsky--Trofimov algorithm. Then, I show how to use the same idea to remove the $\ln \ln T$ factor in the data-independent bound for the Squint algorithm.

</details>


### [107] [Open Problems in Frontier AI Risk Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25982)
*Marta Ziosi,Miro Plueckebaum,Stephen Casper,Henry Papadatos,Ze Shen Chin,Peter Slattery,James Gealy,Tim G. J. Rudner,Brian Tse,Ariel Gil,Patricia Paskov,Maximilian Negele,Rokas Gipiškis,Nada Madkour,Vera Lummis,Rupal Jain,Luise Eder,Kristina Fort,Malou C. van Draanen Glismann,Inès Belhadj,Amin Oueslati,Anna K. Wisakanto,Richard Mallah,Koen Holtman,Ranj Zuhdi,Daniel S. Schiff,Jessica Newman,Malcolm Murray,Robert Trager*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地梳理了前沿AI风险管理中的开放问题，将问题分为三类：科学/技术共识缺失、与现有风险管理框架的错位或挑战、以及实施层面的不足，并识别了应对这些问题的最佳行动者，旨在为未来的研究和治理提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI既放大了现有风险，又引入全新的挑战；技术快速变化导致缺乏稳定的科学共识，且新兴的安全实践往往与现有风险管理框架不一致或削弱其效力，因此需要系统性地梳理和分类这些开放问题。

Method: 采用问题导向的方法，通过结构化文献综述，按照风险管理的五个阶段（风险规划、识别、分析、评估、缓解）逐一分析；将开放问题按三类分类（共识缺失、框架错位、实施不足），并识别最适合应对这些问题的行动者。

Result: 系统性地绘制了前沿AI风险管理中的开放问题图谱，明确了各类问题的性质及应对这些问题的关键行动者（包括开发者、部署者、监管者、标准机构、研究人员和第三方评估者）。

Conclusion: 本文不提出具体解决方案，而是作为一份问题导向的议程设定参考文件，并附带在线实时数据库，旨在支持协调、减少重复工作，并指导未来的研究和治理工作。

Abstract: Frontier AI both amplifies existing risks and introduces qualitatively novel challenges. Not only is there a notable lack of stable scientific consensus resulting from the rapid pace of technological change, but emerging frontier AI safety practices are often misaligned with, or may undermine, established risk management frameworks. To address these challenges, we systematically surface open problems in frontier AI risk management. Adopting a problem-oriented approach, we examine each stage of the risk management process - risk planning, identification, analysis, evaluation, and mitigation - through a structured review of the literature, identifying unresolved challenges and the actors best positioned to address them. Recognising that different types of open problems call for different responses, we classify open problems according to whether they reflect (a) a lack of scientific or technical consensus, (b) misalignment with, or challenges to, established risk management frameworks, or (c) shortcomings in implementation despite apparent consensus and alignment. By mapping these open problems and identifying the actors best positioned to address them - including developers, deployers, regulators, standards bodies, researchers, and third-party evaluators - this work aims to clarify where progress is needed to enable robust and meaningful consensus on frontier AI risk management.The paper does not propose specific solutions; instead, it provides a problem-oriented, agenda-setting reference document, complemented by a living online repository, intended to support coordination, reduce duplication, and guide future research and governance efforts.

</details>


### [108] [Correcting Performance Estimation Bias in Imbalanced Classification with Minority Subconcepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26024)
*Taylor Maxson,Roberto Corizzo,Yaning Wu,Nathalie Japkowicz,Colin Bellinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预测后验概率的实用效用加权评估方法（pBA），用于解决类别层面评估中因子概念分布不均导致的性能评估偏差问题，实验证明该方法在表格、医学影像和文本数据集上比未加权评估更稳定和可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的类别级评估会掩盖同一类别下不同子概念（subconcepts）之间的性能差异，导致平均表现良好的模型在特定子群体上失败。已有的效用加权方法虽然可以缓解这种偏差，但需要真实的子概念标签，而这些标签在测试时几乎不可用。

Method: 提出predicted-weighted balanced accuracy (pBA)：用多类子概念模型的预测后验概率替代不可用的真实子概念标签，定义评估权重为后验概率下的期望效用，从而得到一种软性的、考虑不确定性的加权评估指标。

Result: 在表格基准数据集、医学影像数据集和文本数据集上的实验表明：未加权的评估分数在类内异质性存在时会产生误导性结果，而pBA在子概念分布不均匀但非病理性的情况下提供了更稳定和可解释的评估。

Conclusion: 本文提出的pBA方法有效解决了子概念标签缺失时评估偏差的问题，为存在类内异质性的场景提供了实用且可靠的评估手段。

Abstract: Class-level evaluation can conceal substantial performance disparities across subconcepts within the same class, causing models that perform well on average to fail on specific subpopulations. Prior work has shown that common evaluation measures for imbalanced classification are biased toward larger minority subconcepts and that utility-based reweighting using true subconcept labels can mitigate this bias; however, such labels are rarely available at test time. We introduce a practical utility-weighted evaluation that replaces unavailable subconcept labels with predicted posterior probabilities from a multiclass subconcept model. Evaluation weights are defined as the expected utility under this posterior, yielding a soft, uncertainty-aware metric we call predicted-weighted balanced accuracy (pBA). Experiments on tabular benchmarks as well as medical-imaging and text datasets show that unweighted scores can be misleading under within-class heterogeneity, while pBA provides more stable and interpretable assessments when subconcept distributions are uneven but not pathological. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/correcting-bias-imbalance-9C6C/.

</details>


### [109] [RaMP: Runtime-Aware Megakernel Polymorphism for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26039)
*Vyom Sharma,Debajyoti Datta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RaMP是一个路由感知的MoE推理调度框架，通过性能区域分析和四参数波成本模型，根据运行时专家直方图选择最优内核配置，在无需修改源码的情况下实现1.14x-1.41x的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE推理系统仅根据batch size进行调度，忽略了专家路由分布对最优内核配置的影响，导致10-70%的内核吞吐量未被充分利用。

Method: 提出RaMP框架：1) 性能区域分析仅从硬件常数推导出每种优化何时有效；2) 四参数波成本模型根据运行时专家直方图选择最快配置，仅需单次10-24分钟概要分析即可拟合；3) 该模型仅依赖CTA网格几何，因此与内核无关，可应用于Alpha-MoE（无源码修改得1.14x加速）。

Result: 与穷举搜索相比平均遗憾率0.93%；对所有8个测试架构（含3个未见过的）正确预测；相对静态调度实现1.22x内核加速；在vLLM服务中端到端加速：相对Triton 1.30x，相对DeepGEMM 1.41x，相对FlashInfer CUTLASS 1.13x。

Conclusion: RaMP通过路由感知调度有效释放了MoE推理中因忽略分布信息而损失的吞吐量，其内核无关性设计使其可广泛应用于不同MoE实现，显著提升实际部署性能。

Abstract: The optimal kernel configuration for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference depends on both batch size and the expert routing distribution, yet production systems dispatch from batch size alone, leaving 10-70% of kernel throughput unrealized. We present RaMP, a routing-aware dispatch framework. A performance-region analysis derives, from hardware constants alone, when each optimization helps, correctly predicting all 8 tested architectures, including 3 unseen. A four-parameter wave cost model selects the fastest configuration from the runtime expert histogram, achieving 0.93% mean regret versus exhaustive search, fitted from just 10-24 minutes of one-time profiling per model. Because the model depends only on CTA grid geometry, it is kernel-agnostic: applied to Alpha-MoE, it delivers 1.14x with no source modification. Paired with a co-designed CuTe DSL kernel exposing 134-268 polymorphic configurations, RaMP delivers 1.22x kernel speedup over static dispatch and 1.30x end-to-end speedup in vLLM serving over Triton, 1.41x over DeepGEMM, and 1.13x over FlashInfer CUTLASS.

</details>


### [110] [A Multimodal and Explainable Machine Learning Approach to Diagnosing Multi-Class Ejection Fraction from Electrocardiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25942)
*Catherine Ning,Yu Ma,Cindy Beini Wang,Sean McMahon,Joseph Radojevic,Steven Zweibel,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态机器学习框架，结合12导联心电图时序特征和结构化电子健康记录变量，将左心室射血分数（LVEF）分为四个临床常用分层（正常、轻度、中度、重度降低）。基于36,784对心电图-超声心动图数据训练XGBoost模型，多模态模型在严重降低类别上达到0.95的AUROC，优于单一模态基线，并在时间验证中保持性能稳定，支持在资源有限场景下作为筛查和分诊工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LVEF评估依赖超声心动图，在初级医疗和资源有限环境中可及性受限，需要一种更便捷、低成本的筛查方法。

Method: 1) 构建多模态机器学习框架，融合12导联ECG时序特征和结构化EHR变量；2) 使用XGBoost模型进行四分类LVEF分层（正常>50%、轻度40-50%、中度30-40%、重度<30%）；3) 通过SHAP归因分析识别最具影响力的特征；4) 基于Hartford HealthCare回顾性数据（36,784对ECG-超声心动图，30,952名门诊患者）训练，并在后续19,966份ECG上进行时间泛化性评估。

Result: 多模态模型在one-vs-rest AUROC指标上：严重降低0.95、中度降低0.92、轻度降低0.82、正常0.91，优于ECG-only和EHR-only基线模型，且在时间验证中保持性能稳定。

Conclusion: 基于心电图的多模态LVEF分层可作为实用的筛查和分诊辅助工具，在资源有限的环境中优先安排确认性影像检查。

Abstract: Left ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF) assessment depends on echocardiography, limiting access in primary care and resource-constrained settings. We developed a multimodal machine-learning framework that combines engineered 12-lead ECG timeseries features with structured EHR variables to classify LVEF into four clinically used strata: normal (>50%), mildly reduced (40-50%), moderately reduced (30-40%), and severely reduced (<30%). To support model explainability, we identified the most influential ECG and EHR features via SHAP attributions. Using retrospective data from Hartford HealthCare, we trained XGBoost models on 36,784 ECG-echocardiogram pairs from 30,952 outpatients and evaluated temporal generalizability on 19,966 ECGs from a subsequent period. The multimodal model achieved one-vs-rest AUROCs of 0.95 (severe), 0.92 (moderate), 0.82 (mild), and 0.91 (normal), outperforming ECG-only and EHR-only baselines, and maintained performance under temporal validation. This work supports ECG-based, multimodal LVEF stratification as a practical screening and triage aid to prioritize confirmatory imaging where resources are limited.

</details>


### [111] [Momentum-Conserving Graph Neural Networks for Deformable Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26097)
*Jiahong Wang,Logan Numerow,Stelian Coros,Christian Theobalt,Vahid Babaei,Bernhard Thomaszewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MomentumGNN架构，通过预测每边的拉伸和弯曲冲量来保证线性和角动量守恒，采用无监督物理损失训练，在动量关键场景中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN架构难以正确预测可变形材料动态行为中关键物理量（如线动量和角动量）的时间演化。

Method: 提出MomentumGNN架构，通过预测每边的拉伸和弯曲冲量（而非无约束的节点加速度）来保证线性和角动量守恒，并使用基于物理损失的无监督训练方式。

Result: 在动量起关键作用的多个常见场景中，该方法优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过构造保证动量守恒的GNN架构，结合无监督物理损失训练，能更准确地模拟可变形材料的动态行为。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have emerged as a versatile and efficient option for modeling the dynamic behavior of deformable materials. While GNNs generalize readily to arbitrary shapes, mesh topologies, and material parameters, existing architectures struggle to correctly predict the temporal evolution of key physical quantities such as linear and angular momentum. In this work, we propose MomentumGNN -- a novel architecture designed to accurately track momentum by construction. Unlike existing GNNs that output unconstrained nodal accelerations, our model predicts per-edge stretching and bending impulses which guarantee the preservation of linear and angular momentum. We train our network in an unsupervised fashion using a physics-based loss, and we show that our method outperforms baselines in a number of common scenarios where momentum plays a pivotal role.

</details>


### [112] [reward-lens: A Mechanistic Interpretability Library for Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26130)
*Mohammed Suhail B Nadaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 reward-lens 开源库，将原本用于生成式 LLM 的机械可解释性工具（logit lens、直接 logit 归因、激活修补、稀疏自编码器）迁移至奖励模型。核心洞察是奖励头的权重向量 w_r 可作为所有可解释性问题的自然轴。工具支持奖励透镜、组件归因、三模式激活修补、奖励黑客探测套件、TopK SAE 特征归因、跨模型比较及五项理论扩展。在两个生产级奖励模型、约 695 个 RewardBench 样本上验证，发现线性归因无法预测因果修补效果（Spearman ρ 分别为 -0.256 和 -0.027），并将此分歧视为待暴露的属性而非缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机械可解释性工具专门为基于词汇 unembedding 的生成式 LLM 设计，而奖励模型用标量回归头替代了词汇 unembedding，导致这些工具无法直接适用。作者旨在填补这一空白，为奖励模型提供一套完整的可解释性工具集。

Method: 基于奖励头权重向量 w_r 作为自然分析轴的核心观察，开发了包含奖励透镜、组件归因、三模式激活修补、奖励黑客探测、TopK SAE 特征归因、跨模型比较等工具的开源库。设计了十方法适配器协议覆盖 Llama、Mistral、Gemma-2 和 ArmoRM 多目标头，并提供通用适配器。通过 695 个 RewardBench 样本在两个生产级奖励模型上验证。

Result: 线性归因方法不能预测因果激活修补的效果：Skywork 上平均 Spearman ρ = -0.256，ArmoRM 上为 -0.027。这表明观察性归因与因果性修补之间存在本质分歧。

Conclusion: 该工具框架将线性归因与因果修补之间的不一致视为需要暴露的属性而非需要修复的缺陷，设计上保持观察视角和因果视角均为头等公民且可直接比较，为奖励模型的可解释性研究提供了系统工具。

Abstract: Every RLHF-trained language model is shaped by a reward model, yet the mechanistic interpretability toolkit -- logit lens, direct logit attribution, activation patching, sparse autoencoders -- was built for generative LLMs whose primitives all project onto a vocabulary unembedding. Reward models replace that with a scalar regression head, breaking each tool. We present reward-lens, an open-source library that ports this toolkit to reward models, organised around one observation: the reward head's weight vector $w_r$ is the natural axis for every interpretability question. The library provides a Reward Lens, component attribution, three-mode activation patching, a reward-hacking probe suite, TopK SAE feature attribution, cross-model comparison, and five theory-grounded extensions (distortion index, divergence-aware patching, misalignment cascade detection, reward-term conflict analysis, concept-vector analysis).
